NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_3
------------------------------
Here is the sequence of human-readable actions derived from the plan:

1.  **WRETCHED** (object\_4, object\_7, object\_8, object\_2): Change the 'next' connection for `object_4` (which has the 'sneeze' property) from `object_7` to `object_8`. This requires `object_7` and `object_8` to both have the 'texture' property and collect `object_2` (which is 'stupendous').
2.  **CLIP** (object\_9, object\_4, object\_8): Utilize handheld `object_9` to form a 'vase' connection with `object_4` (which has the 'sneeze' property). Both `object_9` and `object_4` must be 'next' to `object_8` (which has the 'texture' property). This action severs the 'next' connection between `object_9` and `object_8`.
3.  **SIP** (object\_11, object\_0, object\_5): Use handheld `object_11` to establish a 'vase' relationship with `object_0` (which has the 'cats' property). This action requires `object_11` and `object_0` to both be 'next' to `object_5` (which has the 'texture' property). As a result, `object_11` will no longer be 'next' to `object_5`.
4.  **SIP** (object\_10, object\_0, object\_5): Use handheld `object_10` to establish a 'vase' relationship with `object_0` (which has the 'cats' property). This action requires `object_10` and `object_0` to both be 'next' to `object_5` (which has the 'texture' property). As a result, `object_10` will no longer be 'next' to `object_5`.
5.  **WRETCHED** (object\_4, object\_8, object\_7, object\_2): Change the 'next' connection for `object_4` (which has the 'sneeze' property) from `object_8` to `object_7`. This requires `object_8` and `object_7` to both have the 'texture' property and collect `object_2` (which is 'stupendous').
